Un écart qu'aucun document ne porte est une suggestion, pas un bloquant. Classe bloquant/suggestion, rédige un correction_prompt autonome. Un des six reviewers joués en parallèle. Lecture seule ; retourne des findings JSON. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-sdd/.claude/agents/conventions-reviewer.md))
- **conventions-reviewer**: 変更コードの配置・依存関係・規約・既存実装との一貫性・可読性をレビューする。code-review スキルから 3 体のうちの 1 体として起動される ([shin-corp/shinnn-app-plugins](https://github.com/shin-corp/shinnn-app-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/shinnn-app/agents/conventions-reviewer.md))
- **conventions-scout**: Scans the workspace once and writes `.claude/conventions.md` — the project's stack, layering, error-handling, validation, auth, i18n, styling, navigation, test setup, and the exact lint/typecheck/build/test/e2e commands — so every other agent reads one file instead of re-detecting conventions on every story. Called by product-manager at startup when the file is missing, and again in refresh mode every few stories. ([marobert-dev/claude-code](https://github.com/marobert-dev/claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/conventions-scout.md))

- **cookbook-curator**: Surveys the full tdmcp tool surface — layer1/2/3 tools, recipes, CHANGELOG, ROADMAP — and curates 15-20 new prompt cookbook examples that are genuinely surprising or visually impressive and not yet in the cookbook. Output goes to _workspace/cookbook_candidates.md. ([Pantani/tdmcp](https://github.com/Pantani/tdmcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cookbook-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **cookbook-engine**: `@happyvertical/smrt-core/cookbook/engine` (`src/cookbook/engine/`) is the one place the rules for changing a [cookbook](cookbook.md) live. A planner UI, a local Kitchen MCP server, the CLI and a website all call it; each is an adapter. It imports only relative modules and `@happyvertical/smrt-types` types, so it runs in Node and in a browser. No SvelteKit, storage, singleton or inference. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/core/agents/cookbook-engine.md) · ★7)
- **cookbook-host**: The real-HTTP CLI fixture in `__tests__/cookbookHost.test.ts` models a compatible app-tool host for both stream protocols: it accepts the secured flags, preflights successfully, and calls the app MCP endpoint. Claude has built-ins absent; native Codex 0.162.1 now enables its necessary code-mode host and confines incidental patch/command items in disposable workspaces (inference's engine contract). ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/template-sveltekit/agents/cookbook-host.md) · ★7)
- **cookbook-policy**: `resolveFieldPolicy(objectRef, { cookbook, ...context })` inserts the active cookbook rows after stored app rows and before tenant/user tiers, using the same `resolveFieldPolicyLayers` kernel as `./policy`. It bypasses both cache reads and writes so switching cookbook documents never leaks a prior document's policy. - **coopanion-forge-sandbox**: coopanion-forge 运行时验证工程师。按 Cortico profile 搭隔离运行时，真实启动宿主验证 扩展能否被加载、入口是否注册、状态是否健康；失败给归因（扩展缺陷 / 依赖缺失 / 平台 限制 / 沙箱或环境配置错），不写"跑不起来"了事。Use when verifying a Cortico extension actually loads in an isolated host runtime, smoke-testing before delivery, or someone says 宿主里到底跑不跑 / 交付前真实冒烟 / 加载失败排查。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-sandbox.md))
